- **ship-architect**: Define system architecture from approved PRD and research. Produces architecture doc, data model, API contracts, ADRs. Requires user approval. Must run after ship-spec. ([HarambeeAI/bayes-ship](https://github.com/HarambeeAI/bayes-ship/tree/HEAD/skills/ship-architect) · ★1)

- **ship-architect**: Turns an approved spec into a technical design and a decomposed ticket set the factory can build. Use after a PM spec passes its gate and before any ticket is dispatched. Directing-tier: designs and decomposes, does not implement. - **ship-art-pipeline**: Take a ship from concept to an in-game asset through eight owner-approved stages — concept, blockout, detail model, flight check, UV, paint, breakup, lineup review. - **ship-artifact**: Builds, publishes and hands over the per-change Artifact (the one deliverable Łukasz reads after every session) from a fixed template — fills ticket/branch/PR/session links automatically, links it on the board ticket, comments there, moves the ticket to Needs Review and writes the quality-gate marker. ([lukasz512/NeoSleep](https://github.com/lukasz512/NeoSleep/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ship-artifact))
- **ship-as-artifact**: Whenever you finish something a person will read, review, or share — a report, brief, plan, audit, roadmap, comparison, or a design/mockup preview — render it as a self-contained hosted page (e.g. a Claude Artifact) and hand over the link, instead of dumping a wall of terminal markdown or leaving a raw file on disk. - **ship-ask**: Ship workflow (interactive variant): based on the original `/ship`, but with two mandatory AskUserQuestion checkpoints up front — base branch selection (main / develop / other) and base integration strategy (merge / rebase / skip). - **ship-at-end**: Implement the request, then run /ship if all looks good, asking design or clarifying questions up front. Use when the user invokes /ship-at-end. ([raineorshine/dotfiles](https://github.com/raineorshine/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/ship-at-end) · ★9 · 10 copies)
- **ship-at-launch-velocity**: Ship small, launch publicly, kill fast, iterate on live demand. - **ship-audit**: Audit a change before it is committed, pushed or offered for review, and report each gate with the evidence that proves it. - **ship-audit**: Use when asked "is this ready to ship?", "what breaks in prod?", or for a pre-launch or post-merge readiness review of an app or self-hosted service. - **ship-audit**: Audit a whole codebase or product for production readiness. - **ship-audit**: Audits a frontend surface against real budgets before it is called done — Core Web Vitals and bundle size, WCAG 2.2 AA, the state matrix, responsive behavior down to 320px and 200% zoom, and the fallbacks the stack record promised. - **ship-auto**: Auto-ship pipeline — code review, PR, CI, Copilot, merge, deploy — without pausing for human checkpoints.
